*Haotian Ye,Haowei Lin,Jingyi Tang,Yizhen Luo,Caiyin Yang,Chang Su,Rahul Thapa,Rui Yang,Ruihua Liu,Zeyu Li,Chong Gao,Dachao Ding,Guangrong He,Miaolei Zhang,Lina Sun,Wenyang Wang,Yuchen Zhong,Zhuohao Shen,Di He,Jianzhu Ma,Stefano Ermon,Tongyang Li,Xiaowen Chu,James Zou,Yuzhi Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SimpleTES框架通过并行探索、反馈驱动优化和局部选择，系统扩展评估驱动的科学发现循环，在多个科学领域实现突破性成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽然认识到评估在科学发现中的重要性，但缺乏系统化扩展评估驱动发现循环的原则性方法，限制了语言模型在科学发现中的潜力发挥。

Method: 提出SimpleTES框架，战略性地结合并行探索、反馈驱动优化和局部选择三个核心组件，系统扩展评估驱动的发现循环。

Result: 在21个跨6个领域的科学问题上，SimpleTES使用开源模型发现了最先进的解决方案，显著优于前沿模型基准和复杂优化流程。具体成果包括：LASSO算法加速2倍以上、量子电路路由策略减少24.5%门开销、发现超越已知结果的Erdos最小重叠构造。

Conclusion: 有效的评估驱动循环扩展是推进LLM驱动科学发现的核心轴心，SimpleTES提供了一个简单实用的框架实现这些收益，其产生的轨迹历史还能监督反馈驱动学习，提升模型在未见问题上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly used in scientific discovery to generate hypotheses, propose candidate solutions, implement systems, and iteratively refine them. At the core of these trial-and-error loops lies evaluation: the process of obtaining feedback on candidate solutions via verifiers, simulators, or task-specific scoring functions. While prior work has highlighted the importance of evaluation, it has not explicitly formulated the problem of how evaluation-driven discovery loops can be scaled up in a principled and effective manner to push the boundaries of scientific discovery, a problem this paper seeks to address. We introduce Simple Test-time Evaluation-driven Scaling (SimpleTES), a general framework that strategically combines parallel exploration, feedback-driven refinement, and local selection, revealing substantial gains unlocked by scaling evaluation-driven discovery loops along the right dimensions. Across 21 scientific problems spanning six domains, SimpleTES discovers state-of-the-art solutions using gpt-oss models, consistently outperforming both frontier-model baselines and sophisticated optimization pipelines. Particularly, we sped up the widely used LASSO algorithm by over 2x, designed quantum circuit routing policies that reduce gate overhead by 24.5%, and discovered new Erdos minimum overlap constructions that surpass the best-known results. Beyond novel discoveries, SimpleTES produces trajectory-level histories that naturally supervise feedback-driven learning. When post-trained on successful trajectories, models not only improve efficiency on seen problems but also generalize to unseen problems, discovering solutions that base models fail to uncover. Together, our results establish effective evaluation-driven loop scaling as a central axis for advancing LLM-driven scientific discovery, and provide a simple yet practical framework for realizing these gains.

</details>


### [115] [LASER: Learning Active Sensing for Continuum Field Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19355)
*Huayu Deng,Jinghui Zhong,Xiangming Zhu,Yunbo Wang,Xiaokang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LASER是一个用于连续物理场高保真测量的主动感知框架，将主动感知建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，通过强化学习策略在潜在想象空间中模拟"假设"感知场景，实现传感器布局的动态优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续物理场的高保真测量对于科学发现和工程设计至关重要，但在稀疏和受限的传感条件下仍然具有挑战性。传统的重建方法通常依赖于固定的传感器布局，无法适应不断演化的物理状态。

Method: 提出LASER框架，将主动感知建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程。核心是使用连续物理场潜在世界模型捕捉底层物理动力学并提供内在奖励反馈。通过强化学习策略在潜在想象空间中模拟"假设"感知场景，根据预测的潜在状态调节传感器移动，引导传感器朝向潜在高信息区域。

Result: 实验表明，LASER在多种连续物理场中始终优于静态和离线优化策略，在稀疏条件下实现了高保真重建。

Conclusion: LASER提供了一个统一的闭环框架，通过将主动感知建模为POMDP并利用潜在世界模型和强化学习，实现了对连续物理场的自适应高保真测量，解决了传统固定传感器布局方法的局限性。

Abstract: High-fidelity measurements of continuum physical fields are essential for scientific discovery and engineering design but remain challenging under sparse and constrained sensing. Conventional reconstruction methods typically rely on fixed sensor layouts, which cannot adapt to evolving physical states. We propose LASER, a unified, closed-loop framework that formulates active sensing as a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP). At its core, LASER employs a continuum field latent world model that captures the underlying physical dynamics and provides intrinsic reward feedback. This enables a reinforcement learning policy to simulate ''what-if'' sensing scenarios within a latent imagination space. By conditioning sensor movements on predicted latent states, LASER navigates toward potentially high-information regions beyond current observations. Our experiments demonstrate that LASER consistently outperforms static and offline-optimized strategies, achieving high-fidelity reconstruction under sparsity across diverse continuum fields.

</details>


### [116] [FairTree: Subgroup Fairness Auditing of Machine Learning Models with Bias-Variance Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19357)
*Rudolf Debelak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairTree：一种基于心理测量不变性测试的新算法，用于检测机器学习模型在子组中的性能差异，直接处理连续、分类和有序特征，无需离散化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习模型评估主要依赖基于损失函数的性能指标，可能忽略相关子组中的性能变化。现有审计工具如SliceFinder和SliceLine存在概念缺陷，特别是无法直接处理连续协变量

Method: 提出FairTree算法，改编自心理测量不变性测试。算法直接处理连续、分类和有序特征，无需离散化，并将性能差异分解为系统偏差和方差。提出两种变体：基于排列的方法（概念上更接近SliceFinder）和波动测试

Result: 通过模拟研究（包括与SliceLine的直接比较）表明，两种方法都有令人满意的假阳性率，但波动测试方法具有相对更高的统计功效。在UCI成人人口普查数据集上进行了方法演示

Conclusion: FairTree算法为统计评估机器学习模型的性能和公平性方面提供了一个灵活框架，即使在相对较小的数据集中也能在广泛应用中发挥作用

Abstract: The evaluation of machine learning models typically relies mainly on performance metrics based on loss functions, which risk to overlook changes in performance in relevant subgroups. Auditing tools such as SliceFinder and SliceLine were proposed to detect such groups, but usually have conceptual disadvantages, such as the inability to directly address continuous covariates. In this paper, we introduce FairTree, a novel algorithm adapted from psychometric invariance testing. Unlike SliceFinder and related algorithms, FairTree directly handles continuous, categorical, and ordinal features without discretization. It further decomposes performance disparities into systematic bias and variance, allowing a categorization of changes in algorithm performance. We propose and evaluate two variations of the algorithm: a permutation-based approach, which is conceptually closer to SliceFinder, and a fluctuation test. Through simulation studies that include a direct comparison with SliceLine, we demonstrate that both approaches have a satisfactory rate of false-positive results, but that the fluctuation approach has relatively higher power. We further illustrate the method on the UCI Adult Census dataset. The proposed algorithms provide a flexible framework for the statistical evaluation of the performance and aspects of fairness of machine learning models in a wide range of applications even in relatively small data.

</details>


### [117] [TACENR: Task-Agnostic Contrastive Explanations for Node Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19372)
*Vasiliki Papanikou,Evaggelia Pitoura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TACENR是一种任务无关的局部解释方法，用于解释节点表示，通过对比学习识别属性、邻近性和结构特征对表示空间的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表示学习方法虽然成功，但节点表示不透明且难以解释。现有解释方法主要关注监督设置或解释单个表示维度，缺乏对节点表示整体结构的解释。

Method: 提出TACENR方法，基于对比学习学习表示空间中的相似性函数，识别对节点表示最重要的属性特征、邻近性特征和结构特征。该方法主要针对任务无关解释，也可应用于监督场景。

Result: 实验结果表明邻近性和结构特征在塑造节点表示中起重要作用，且监督变体在识别最具影响力特征方面与现有任务特定方法表现相当。

Conclusion: TACENR填补了图表示学习解释性方面的关键空白，能够解释节点表示的整体结构，为理解图表示学习提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Graph representation learning has achieved notable success in encoding graph-structured data into latent vector spaces, enabling a wide range of downstream tasks. However, these node representations remain opaque and difficult to interpret. Existing explainability methods primarily focus on supervised settings or on explaining individual representation dimensions, leaving a critical gap in explaining the overall structure of node representations. In this paper, we propose TACENR (Task-Agnostic Contrastive Explanations for Node Representations), a local explanation method that identifies not only attribute features but also proximity and structural ones that contribute the most in the representation space. TACENR builds on contrastive learning, through which we learn a similarity function in the representation space, revealing which are the features that play an important role in the representation of a node. While our focus is on task-agnostic explanations, TACENR can be applied to supervised scenarios as well. Experimental results demonstrate that proximity and structural features play a significant role in shaping node representations and that our supervised variant performs comparably to existing task-specific approaches in identifying the most impactful features.

</details>


### [118] [Revisiting Catastrophic Forgetting in Continual Knowledge Graph Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19401)
*Gerard Pons,Carlos Escolano,Besim Bilalli,Anna Queralt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出当前连续知识图谱嵌入方法忽略了实体干扰问题，导致对灾难性遗忘的评估存在偏差，并提出了修正的评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前连续知识图谱嵌入方法主要关注限制现有嵌入的变化来应对灾难性遗忘，但忽略了新引入实体对已学习实体的干扰问题，导致评估不准确且性能被系统性高估。

Method: 1. 识别并定义实体干扰现象；2. 提出修正的CKGE评估协议以考虑实体干扰；3. 在多个基准测试上进行实验验证；4. 分析不同CKGE方法和KGE模型受遗忘源的影响；5. 引入专门针对CKGE的灾难性遗忘度量指标。

Result: 忽略实体干扰会导致性能被高估高达25%，特别是在实体显著增长的情况下。修正的评估协议揭示了当前CKGE方法性能被系统性高估的问题，并提供了更准确的评估框架。

Conclusion: 实体干扰是CKGE中一个被忽视但重要的现象，必须纳入评估协议才能准确衡量灾难性遗忘。提出的修正评估协议和专门度量指标为CKGE研究提供了更可靠的评估基础。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph Embeddings (KGEs) support a wide range of downstream tasks over Knowledge Graphs (KGs). In practice, KGs evolve as new entities and facts are added, motivating Continual Knowledge Graph Embedding (CKGE) methods that update embeddings over time. Current CKGE approaches address catastrophic forgetting (i.e., the performance degradation on previously learned tasks) primarily by limiting changes to existing embeddings.
  However, we show that this view is incomplete. When new entities are introduced, their embeddings can interfere with previously learned ones, causing the model to predict them in place of previously correct answers. This phenomenon, which we call entity interference, has been largely overlooked and is not accounted for in current CKGE evaluation protocols. As a result, the assessment of catastrophic forgetting becomes misleading, and CKGE methods performance is systematically overestimated.
  To address this issue, we introduce a corrected CKGE evaluation protocol that accounts for entity interference. Through experiments on multiple benchmarks, we show that ignoring this effect can lead to performance overestimation of up to 25%, particularly in scenarios with significant entity growth. We further analyze how different CKGE methods and KGE models are affected by the different sources of forgetting, and introduce a catastrophic forgetting metric tailored to CKGE.

</details>


### [119] [Heterogeneity-Aware Personalized Federated Learning for Industrial Predictive Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19451)
*Yuhan Hu,Xiaolei Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出个性化联邦预测模型，解决传统联邦模型假设退化过程同质性的问题，允许异构退化过程的客户端构建定制化预测模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦预测模型通常假设客户端间退化过程同质，但工业场景中退化过程往往存在异质性，需要个性化模型来适应不同客户端的特定退化模式

Method: 开发个性化联邦预测模型，通过迭代促进具有相似退化模式的客户端间成对协作，基于近端梯度下降开发联邦参数估计算法，使用分散数据集联合估计参数

Result: 通过大量仿真研究和NASA涡轮风扇发动机退化数据集案例验证了所提模型的优越性，能够同时实现模型个性化、保护数据隐私并提供全面的失效时间分布

Conclusion: 提出的个性化联邦预测模型克服了现有联邦预测模型的局限性，为具有异构退化过程的工业场景提供了有效的解决方案

Abstract: Federated prognostics enable clients (e.g., companies, factories, and production lines) to collaboratively develop a failure time prediction model while keeping each client's data local and confidential. However, traditional federated models often assume homogeneity in the degradation processes across clients, an assumption that may not hold in many industrial settings. To overcome this, this paper proposes a personalized federated prognostic model designed to accommodate clients with heterogeneous degradation processes, allowing them to build tailored prognostic models. The prognostic model iteratively facilitates the underlying pairwise collaborations between clients with similar degradation patterns, which enhances the performance of personalized federated learning. To estimate parameters jointly using decentralized datasets, we develop a federated parameter estimation algorithm based on proximal gradient descent. The proposed approach addresses the limitations of existing federated prognostic models by simultaneously achieving model personalization, preserving data privacy, and providing comprehensive failure time distributions. The superiority of the proposed model is validated through extensive simulation studies and a case study using the turbofan engine degradation dataset from the NASA repository.

</details>


### [120] [EVPO: Explained Variance Policy Optimization for Adaptive Critic Utilization in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19485)
*Chengjun Pan,Shichun Liu,Jiahang Lin,Dingwei Zhu,Jiazheng Zhang,Shihan Dou,Songyang Gao,Zhenhua Han,Binghai Wang,Rui Zheng,Xuanjing Huang,Tao Gui,Yansong Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EVPO：一种基于解释方差的自适应基线选择方法，在稀疏奖励场景下通过监控批量级解释方差，动态切换基于评论家与批量均值优势估计，实现比PPO和GRPO更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RL理论支持使用学习评论家作为基线以减少方差，但在稀疏奖励设置中，评论家的估计噪声可能超过其捕获的状态信号，反而增加优势方差。需要一种理论指导的方法来判定何时使用评论家基线更有效。

Method: 将基线选择建模为卡尔曼滤波问题，统一PPO和GRPO作为卡尔曼增益的两个极端。提出解释方差(EV)作为判别标准：正EV表示评论家减少方差，零或负EV表示增加方差。基于此提出EVPO算法，在每一步训练中监控批量级EV，自适应地在基于评论家和批量均值优势估计之间切换。

Result: 在经典控制、智能体交互和数学推理四个任务上，EVPO始终优于PPO和GRPO，无论哪个固定基线在特定任务上更强。分析证实自适应门控能跟踪评论家在训练中的成熟度，且理论推导的零阈值在经验上是最优的。

Conclusion: 解释方差为基线选择提供了理论依据和实用标准，EVPO通过自适应切换机制实现了比固定基线方法更优的方差控制和性能表现，解决了稀疏奖励场景下评论家基线可能适得其反的问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for LLM post-training faces a fundamental design choice: whether to use a learned critic as a baseline for policy optimization. Classical theory favors critic-based methods such as PPO for variance reduction, yet critic-free alternatives like GRPO have gained widespread adoption due to their simplicity and competitive performance. We show that in sparse-reward settings, a learned critic can inject estimation noise that exceeds the state signal it captures, increasing rather than reducing advantage variance. By casting baseline selection as a Kalman filtering problem, we unify PPO and GRPO as two extremes of the Kalman gain and prove that explained variance (EV), computable from a single training batch, identifies the exact boundary: positive EV indicates the critic reduces variance, while zero or negative EV signals that it inflates variance. Building on this insight, we propose Explained Variance Policy Optimization (EVPO), which monitors batch-level EV at each training step and adaptively switches between critic-based and batch-mean advantage estimation, provably achieving no greater variance than the better of the two at every step. Across four tasks spanning classical control, agentic interaction, and mathematical reasoning, EVPO consistently outperforms both PPO and GRPO regardless of which fixed baseline is stronger on a given task. Further analysis confirms that the adaptive gating tracks critic maturation over training and that the theoretically derived zero threshold is empirically optimal.

</details>


### [121] [When Graph Structure Becomes a Liability: A Critical Re-Evaluation of Graph Neural Networks for Bitcoin Fraud Detection under Temporal Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19514)
*Saket Maganti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过严格的防泄漏评估协议挑战了GNN在Elliptic比特币数据集上优于特征基线方法的共识，发现随机森林在原始特征上表现最佳，而GNN的性能优势主要源于训练时暴露测试期邻接信息的数据泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是验证广泛引用的共识——GCN、GraphSAGE、GAT和EvolveGCN在Elliptic比特币数据集上优于仅使用特征的基线方法——是否能在无泄漏的严格评估协议下成立。作者认为这一共识缺乏在防泄漏条件下的压力测试。

Method: 采用种子匹配的归纳与转导对比方法，设计严格的归纳协议防止数据泄漏。通过配对控制实验量化训练时暴露测试期邻接信息的影响，进行边洗牌消融实验评估图拓扑的真实价值，并测试结合GNN嵌入与原始特征的混合模型。

Result: 在严格归纳协议下，随机森林在原始特征上达到F1=0.821，优于所有评估的GNN（GraphSAGE仅达到F1=0.689）。配对实验显示39.5个F1分数点的差距可归因于训练时暴露测试期邻接信息。边洗牌实验表明随机连边的图优于真实交易图，说明数据集拓扑在时间分布偏移下具有误导性。混合模型仅提供边际增益且仍显著低于特征基线。

Conclusion: 论文结论是先前关于GNN在Elliptic比特币数据集上优于特征基线的共识不成立，其性能优势主要源于数据泄漏而非图拓扑的真实价值。作者强调需要严格的防泄漏评估协议，并发布了代码、检查点和严格归纳协议以确保可重复的无泄漏评估。

Abstract: The consensus that GCN, GraphSAGE, GAT, and EvolveGCN outperform feature-only baselines on the Elliptic Bitcoin Dataset is widely cited but has not been rigorously stress-tested under a leakage-free evaluation protocol. We perform a seed-matched inductive-versus-transductive comparison and find that this consensus does not hold. Under a strictly inductive protocol, Random Forest on raw features achieves F1 = 0.821 and outperforms all evaluated GNNs, while GraphSAGE reaches F1 = 0.689 +/- 0.017. A paired controlled experiment reveals a 39.5-point F1 gap attributable to training-time exposure to test-period adjacency. Additionally, edge-shuffle ablations show that randomly wired graphs outperform the real transaction graph, indicating that the dataset's topology can be misleading under temporal distribution shift. Hybrid models combining GNN embeddings with raw features provide only marginal gains and remain substantially below feature-only baselines. We release code, checkpoints, and a strict-inductive protocol to enable reproducible, leakage-free evaluation.

</details>


### [122] [Revisiting RaBitQ and TurboQuant: A Symmetric Comparison of Methods, Theory, and Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19528)
*Jianyang Gao,Yutong Gou,Yuexuan Xu,Jifan Shi,Yongyi Yang,Shuolin Li,Raymond Chi-Wing Wong,Cheng Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在统一框架下重新比较RaBitQ和TurboQuant两种量化方法，发现TurboQuant并未提供一致改进，且在多数测试配置中表现更差，同时指出TurboQuant论文中的实验结果存在可复现性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视RaBitQ和TurboQuant两种量化方法的关系，建立统一、可复现、透明的比较框架，澄清两种方法之间的真实差异，同时验证TurboQuant论文中声称的优势是否成立。

Method: 采用可复现、透明且对称的实验设置，从方法论、理论保证和实证性能三个维度对RaBitQ和TurboQuant进行系统比较，包括在相同配置下测试两种方法的性能表现。

Result: TurboQuant并未提供相对于RaBitQ的一致改进，在多数测试配置中表现更差；TurboQuant论文中报告的运行时间和召回率结果在给定配置下无法从发布的实现中复现。

Conclusion: 本文澄清了两种方法的共享结构和真实差异，同时记录了TurboQuant论文中实验结果的复现性问题，表明TurboQuant声称的优势在实际可比设置中并不成立。

Abstract: This technical note revisits the relationship between RaBitQ and TurboQuant under a unified comparison framework. We compare the two methods in terms of methodology, theoretical guarantees, and empirical performance, using a reproducible, transparent, and symmetric setup. Our results show that, despite the claimed advantage of TurboQuant, TurboQuant does not provide a consistent improvement over RaBitQ in directly comparable settings; in many tested configurations, it performs worse than RaBitQ. We further find that several reported runtime and recall results in the TurboQuant paper could not be reproduced from the released implementation under the stated configuration. Overall, this note clarifies the shared structure and genuine differences between the two lines of work, while documenting reproducibility issues in the experimental results reported by the TurboQuant paper.

</details>


### [123] [Calibrating Scientific Foundation Models with Inference-Time Stochastic Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19530)
*Akash Yadav,Taiwo A. Adebiyi,Ruda Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Stochastic Attention方法，通过随机化注意力机制实现预测不确定性校准，无需重新训练即可生成预测集成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer基础模型在科学应用中需要确定性输出和校准的预测不确定性，但当前架构缺乏对不确定性校准的支持

Method: 提出Stochastic Attention：轻量级推理时修改，用归一化多项分布样本替换softmax权重，通过单一浓度参数控制随机性；引入校准目标匹配输出与目标，形成高效单变量后验调优问题

Result: 在天气和时间序列预测等科学基础模型上评估，相比不确定性感知基线，Stochastic Attention实现最强原生校准和最尖锐预测区间，仅需分钟级后验调优而非基线所需的天级重新训练

Conclusion: Stochastic Attention为Transformer基础模型提供高效、轻量的不确定性校准方法，显著提升预测可靠性同时保持计算效率

Abstract: Transformer-based scientific foundation models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings, but current architectures give deterministic outputs and provide limited support for calibrated predictive uncertainty. We propose Stochastic Attention, a lightweight inference-time modification that randomizes attention by replacing softmax weights with normalized multinomial samples controlled by a single concentration parameter, and produces predictive ensembles without retraining. To set this parameter, we introduce a calibration objective that matches the stochastic attention output with the target, yielding an efficient univariate post-hoc tuning problem. We evaluate this mechanism on two scientific foundation models for weather and timeseries forecasting along with an additional regression task. Across benchmarks against uncertainty-aware baselines, we find that Stochastic Attention achieves the strongest native calibration and the sharpest prediction intervals at comparable coverage, while requiring only minutes of post-hoc tuning versus days of retraining for competitive baselines.

</details>


### [124] [Structure-guided molecular design with contrastive 3D protein-ligand learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19562)
*Carles Navarro,Philipp Tholke,Gianni de Fabritiis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一框架结合对比3D结构编码与自回归分子生成，解决基于结构药物发现中准确捕获蛋白质-配体相互作用与导航超大规模化学空间的双重挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于结构的药物发现面临双重挑战：1) 需要准确捕获3D蛋白质-配体相互作用；2) 需要在超大规模化学空间中导航以识别可合成候选物。现有方法难以同时解决这两个问题

Method: 1) 引入SE(3)-等变transformer，通过对比学习将配体和口袋结构编码到共享嵌入空间；2) 将这些嵌入集成到多模态化学语言模型(MCLM)中，该模型基于口袋或配体结构生成靶标特异性分子，使用学习的数据集令牌引导输出到目标化学空间

Result: 1) 在零样本虚拟筛选中取得竞争性结果；2) 模型生成具有有利预测结合特性的候选分子，适用于多种靶标

Conclusion: 该统一框架成功解决了基于结构药物发现的双重挑战，通过结合对比3D结构编码和条件分子生成，能够有效导航商业化合物空间并产生具有良好结合特性的靶标特异性分子

Abstract: Structure-based drug discovery faces the dual challenge of accurately capturing 3D protein-ligand interactions while navigating ultra-large chemical spaces to identify synthetically accessible candidates. In this work, we present a unified framework that addresses these challenges by combining contrastive 3D structure encoding with autoregressive molecular generation conditioned on commercial compound spaces. First, we introduce an SE(3)-equivariant transformer that encodes ligand and pocket structures into a shared embedding space via contrastive learning, achieving competitive results in zero-shot virtual screening. Second, we integrate these embeddings into a multimodal Chemical Language Model (MCLM). The model generates target-specific molecules conditioned on either pocket or ligand structures, with a learned dataset token that steers the output toward targeted chemical spaces, yielding candidates with favorable predicted binding properties across diverse targets.

</details>


### [125] [An Efficient Black-Box Reduction from Online Learning to Multicalibration, and a New Route to $Φ$-Regret Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19592)
*Gabriele Farina,Juan Carlos Perdomo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了从在线学习到在线多校准的GGM风格黑盒归约，证明了高效多校准与EVI求解的等价性，并建立了从外部遗憾到Φ-遗憾的新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Garg等人(SODA '24)提出的主要开放问题：证明具有√T型保证的oracle高效在线多校准在完全一般性下是可能的。统一现有在线多校准算法的分析，并为具有延迟观测或删失结果的挑战性环境提供新算法。

Method: 1. 提出Gordon-Greenwald-Marks风格的从在线学习到在线多校准的黑盒归约，将任何无遗憾学习器与期望变分不等式(EVI)求解器结合；2. 证明高效多校准与高效EVI求解的等价性；3. 建立从高维在线多校准到(上下文)Φ-遗憾最小化的细粒度归约。

Result: 1. 完全解决了Garg等人(SODA '24)的主要开放问题；2. 统一了现有在线多校准算法的分析；3. 为延迟观测和删失结果环境提供了新算法；4. 实现了在线学习与多类全能预测之间的首次高效黑盒归约；5. 简化了Daskalakis等人(STOC '25)的结果并改进了收敛速率；6. 产生了对更丰富偏差类(如再生核希尔伯特空间)鲁棒的新算法。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了在线学习与在线多校准之间的深刻联系，通过GGM风格归约统一了多个领域，为在线公平性学习提供了新的理论框架和实用算法，并开辟了从外部遗憾到Φ-遗憾的新研究路径。

Abstract: We give a Gordon-Greenwald-Marks (GGM) style black-box reduction from online learning to online multicalibration. Concretely, we show that to achieve high-dimensional multicalibration with respect to a class of functions H, it suffices to combine any no-regret learner over H with an expected variational inequality (EVI) solver. We also prove a converse statement showing that efficient multicalibration implies efficient EVI solving, highlighting how EVIs in multicalibration mirror the role of fixed points in the GGM result for $Φ$-regret.
  This first set of results resolves the main open question in Garg, Jung, Reingold, and Roth (SODA '24), showing that oracle-efficient online multicalibration with $\sqrt{T}$-type guarantees is possible in full generality. Furthermore, our GGM-style reduction unifies the analyses of existing online multicalibration algorithms, enables new algorithms for challenging environments with delayed observations or censored outcomes, and yields the first efficient black-box reduction between online learning and multiclass omniprediction.
  Our second main result is a fine-grained reduction from high-dimensional online multicalibration to (contextual) $Φ$-regret minimization. Together with our first result, this establishes a new route from external regret to Phi-regret that bypasses sophisticated fixed-point or semi-separation machinery, dramatically simplifies a result of Daskalakis, Farina, Fishelson, Pipis, and Schneider (STOC '25) while improving rates, and yields new algorithms that are robust to richer deviation classes, such as those belonging to any reproducing kernel Hilbert space.

</details>


### [126] [SAGE: Training-Free Semantic Evidence Composition for Edge-Cloud Inference under Hard Uplink Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19623)
*Inhyeok Choi,Hyuncheol Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出SAGE方法，在边缘-云混合推理中通过语义注意力引导的证据选择，在硬比特预算约束下优化传输内容，超越传统仅基于注意力的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘-云混合推理中，上行信道对传输比特数有硬约束。传统基于注意力重要性的内容选择方法在硬预算下存在固有局限性，需要更有效的传输策略。

Method: 提出SAGE（语义注意力引导证据）方法，结合重要性过滤和嵌入多样性采样。该方法无需训练，通过语义覆盖而非单纯重要性来选择传输内容。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上，SAGE仅传输不到一半可用证据单元就达到服务器上限精度的93%，显著优于仅基于重要性的方法。

Conclusion: 在硬预算约束下，传输内容的选择应注重语义覆盖而非单纯重要性，SAGE方法通过结合重要性过滤和多样性采样实现了这一目标。

Abstract: Edge-cloud hybrid inference offloads difficult inputs to a powerful remote model, but the uplink channel imposes hard per-request constraints on the number of bits that can be transmitted. We show that selecting transmitted content based solely on attention-based importance, the standard approach in collaborative inference, is inherently limited under hard budgets. Two findings support this claim. First, replacing high-importance units with low-importance but complementary ones improves server accuracy. This shows that what matters is not individual importance but how well the transmitted set covers diverse aspects of the input. Second, spatially uniform selection without any content information achieves competitive accuracy at moderate budgets. This confirms that spatial coverage alone carries independent value. Based on this analysis, we propose SAGE (Semantic Attention-Guided Evidence), a principled, training-free method that combines importance filtering with embedding-diversity sampling. SAGE achieves 93% of the server ceiling in offloaded accuracy while transmitting fewer than half of the available evidence units on ImageNet-1K, substantially outperforming importance-only composition.

</details>


### [127] [Disentangling Damage from Operational Variability: A Label-Free Self-Supervised Representation Learning Framework for Output-Only Structural Damage Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19658)
*Xudong Jian,Charikleia Stoura,Simon Scandella,Eleni Chatzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种自监督无标签解耦表示学习框架，用于基于振动的结构损伤识别，通过分离损伤相关特征与干扰变量，提高实际应用中的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构健康监测中的损伤识别在实际应用中可靠性常受非损伤因素（如激励和环境条件变化）影响，这些因素引起的变化可能等于或大于结构损伤引起的变化，需要开发对损伤敏感但对干扰变量不变的鲁棒识别方法。

Method: 采用具有两个潜在表示的自编码器直接从原始振动加速度信号学习。通过VICReg自监督不变性正则化对基线数据施加约束，假设结构损伤恒定但操作和环境条件变化。引入频域约束强制潜在表示重建的功率谱密度与输入时间序列计算的功率谱密度一致。这些机制共同促进解耦表示学习。

Result: 在两个不同的真实世界振动数据集（桥梁和齿轮箱）上验证了有效性。结果表明该方法对操作变化具有鲁棒性，具有强大的泛化能力，在损伤检测和量化方面表现良好。

Conclusion: 提出的自监督无标签解耦表示学习框架能够有效分离损伤相关特征与干扰变量，实现端到端训练且无需损伤、激励或环境条件的先验信息，适合实际应用，为基于振动的结构损伤识别提供了鲁棒解决方案。

Abstract: Damage identification is a core task in structural health monitoring. In practice, however, its reliability is often compromised by confounding non-damage effects, such as variations in excitation and environmental conditions, which can induce changes comparable to or larger than those caused by structural damage. To address this challenge, this study proposes a self-supervised label-free disentangled representation learning framework for robust vibration-based structural damage identification. The proposed framework employs an autoencoder with two latent representations to learn directly from raw vibration acceleration signals. A self-supervised invariance regularization, implemented via Variance-Invariance-Covariance Regularization (VICReg), is imposed on one latent representation using baseline data where structural damage is assumed constant but operational and environmental conditions vary. In addition, a frequency-domain constraint is introduced to enforce agreement between the power spectral density reconstructed from the latent representation and that computed from the corresponding input time series. Together, these mechanisms promote disentanglement, enabling the learned representation to be sensitive to damage-related characteristics while remaining invariant to nuisance variability. The framework is trained in a fully end-to-end and label-free manner, requiring no prior information on damage, excitation, or environmental conditions, making it well-suited for real-world applications. Its effectiveness is validated on two distinct real-world vibration datasets, including a bridge and a gearbox. The results demonstrate robustness to operational variability, strong generalization capability, and good performance in both damage detection and quantification.

</details>


### [128] [Budgeted Online Influence Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19672)
*Pierre Perrault,Jennifer Healey,Zheng Wen,Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出新的预算约束在线影响力最大化框架，用总成本替代传统基数约束，更贴合现实广告场景，提供理论与实验验证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线影响力最大化通常采用基数约束（选择固定数量的影响者），但现实中影响者成本各异，广告主更关注在总预算下获得最佳投资回报

Method: 基于独立级联传播模型和边级半赌博反馈，提出新算法处理预算约束问题，同时适用于传统基数约束场景

Result: 算法在预算约束下表现良好，在基数约束场景下改进了现有最优遗憾界，实验验证了方法的有效性

Conclusion: 预算约束框架更贴合实际广告需求，提出的算法在理论和实验上都表现优异，为在线影响力最大化提供了更实用的解决方案

Abstract: We introduce a new budgeted framework for online influence maximization, considering the total cost of an advertising campaign instead of the common cardinality constraint on a chosen influencer set. Our approach better models the real-world setting where the cost of influencers varies and advertisers want to find the best value for their overall social advertising budget. We propose an algorithm assuming an independent cascade diffusion model and edge level semi-bandit feedback, and provide both theoretical and experimental results. Our analysis is also valid for the cardinality constraint setting and improves the state of the art regret bound in this case.

</details>


### [129] [PREF-XAI: Preference-Based Personalized Rule Explanations of Black-Box Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19684)
*Salvatore Greco,Jacek Karolczak,Roman Słowiński,Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于偏好的可解释人工智能(PREF-XAI)框架，将解释视为偏好驱动的决策问题，通过形式化偏好学习为用户提供个性化解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法主要生成模型中心的解释，忽略了不同用户因目标、偏好和认知约束需要不同解释这一根本问题。虽然近期研究探索了用户中心和个性化解释，但大多依赖启发式调整或隐式用户建模，缺乏表示和学习个体偏好的原则性框架。

Method: 提出PREF-XAI框架，将解释重构为偏好驱动的决策问题。结合基于规则的解释和形式化偏好学习，通过用户对少量候选解释的排序来获取偏好，使用鲁棒序数回归推断加性效用函数来建模用户偏好。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，PREF-XAI能够从有限反馈中准确重建用户偏好，识别高度相关的解释，并发现用户最初未考虑的新解释规则。

Conclusion: PREF-XAI建立了XAI与偏好学习之间的联系，为交互式和自适应解释系统开辟了新方向，强调解释应作为根据用户特定标准评估和选择的备选方案而非固定输出。

Abstract: Explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) has predominantly focused on generating model-centric explanations that approximate the behavior of black-box models. However, such explanations often overlook a fundamental aspect of interpretability: different users require different explanations depending on their goals, preferences, and cognitive constraints. Although recent work has explored user-centric and personalized explanations, most existing approaches rely on heuristic adaptations or implicit user modeling, lacking a principled framework for representing and learning individual preferences. In this paper, we consider Preference-Based Explainable Artificial Intelligence (PREF-XAI), a novel perspective that reframes explanation as a preference-driven decision problem. Within PREF-XAI, explanations are not treated as fixed outputs, but as alternatives to be evaluated and selected according to user-specific criteria. In the PREF-XAI perspective, here we propose a methodology that combines rule-based explanations with formal preference learning. User preferences are elicited through a ranking of a small set of candidate explanations and modeled via an additive utility function inferred using robust ordinal regression. Experimental results on real-world datasets show that PREF-XAI can accurately reconstruct user preferences from limited feedback, identify highly relevant explanations, and discover novel explanatory rules not initially considered by the user. Beyond the proposed methodology, this work establishes a connection between XAI and preference learning, opening new directions for interactive and adaptive explanation systems.

</details>


### [130] [Planning in entropy-regularized Markov decision processes and games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19695)
*Jean-Bastien Grill,Omar Darwiche Domingues,Pierre Ménard,Rémi Munos,Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SmoothCruiser是一种用于估计熵正则化MDP和双人博弈价值函数的新规划算法，在生成模型环境下实现O~(1/ε⁴)的样本复杂度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非正则化设置中，没有已知算法能在最坏情况下保证多项式样本复杂度。熵正则化通过促进Bellman算子的平滑性，为开发具有理论保证的高效算法提供了机会

Method: SmoothCruiser利用熵正则化带来的Bellman算子平滑性，设计了一种新的规划算法。该算法在生成模型环境下工作，通过正则化实现更稳定的价值函数估计

Result: 算法实现了问题无关的样本复杂度O~(1/ε⁴)，其中ε为期望精度。这是首个在最坏情况下具有多项式样本复杂度保证的算法，相比非正则化设置的未知复杂度有显著改进

Conclusion: 熵正则化为强化学习中的规划问题提供了理论优势，SmoothCruiser算法证明了正则化可以带来更好的样本复杂度保证，为开发具有理论保证的高效算法开辟了新途径

Abstract: We propose SmoothCruiser, a new planning algorithm for estimating the value function in entropy-regularized Markov decision processes and two-player games, given a generative model of the environment. SmoothCruiser makes use of the smoothness of the Bellman operator promoted by the regularization to achieve problem-independent sample complexity of order O~(1/epsilon^4) for a desired accuracy epsilon, whereas for non-regularized settings there are no known algorithms with guaranteed polynomial sample complexity in the worst case.

</details>


### [131] [On two ways to use determinantal point processes for Monte Carlo integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19698)
*Guillaume Gautier,Rémi Bardenet,Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了基于行列式点过程（DPP）的蒙特卡洛积分估计器，比较了两种现有方法：Bardenet & Hardy（2020）的固定DPP方法和Ermakov & Zolotukhin（1960）的定制DPP方法，并将它们推广到连续设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准蒙特卡洛估计器使用独立样本，方差为O(1/N)。使用具有排斥性质的行列式点过程（DPP）作为样本可以改进估计性能，但现有DPP方法各有局限：Bardenet & Hardy的方法使用固定DPP，Ermakov & Zolotukhin的方法需要针对特定函数定制DPP。需要系统比较和推广这些方法。

Method: 重新审视两种DPP基估计器：1) Bardenet & Hardy（2020）方法：使用固定DPP，对光滑函数f达到O(N^{-(1+1/d)})的方差收敛速率；2) Ermakov & Zolotukhin（1960）方法：使用针对f定制的DPP，获得无偏估计，方差为O(1/N)。论文将这些方法推广到连续设置，并提供相应的采样算法。

Result: 系统比较了两种DPP基估计器的性能特征：固定DPP方法在光滑函数条件下提供更快的收敛速率O(N^{-(1+1/d)})，而定制DPP方法保持无偏性且方差与标准蒙特卡洛相当（O(1/N)）。提供了连续设置的推广和实际采样算法。

Conclusion: DPP基估计器为蒙特卡洛积分提供了有前景的替代方案，在方差收敛速率方面可能优于标准蒙特卡洛。两种方法各有优势：固定DPP方法在光滑函数条件下收敛更快，定制DPP方法保持无偏性。推广到连续设置和提供采样算法使这些方法更具实用性。

Abstract: The standard Monte Carlo estimator $\widehat{I}_N^{\mathrm{MC}}$ of $\int fdω$ relies on independent samples from $ω$ and has variance of order $1/N$. Replacing the samples with a determinantal point process (DPP), a repulsive distribution, makes the estimator consistent, with variance rates that depend on how the DPP is adapted to $f$ and $ω$. We examine two existing DPP-based estimators: one by Bardenet & Hardy (2020) with a rate of $\mathcal{O}(N^{-(1+1/d)})$ for smooth $f$, but relying on a fixed DPP. The other, by Ermakov & Zolotukhin (1960), is unbiased with rate of order $1/N$, like Monte Carlo, but its DPP is tailored to $f$. We revisit these estimators, generalize them to continuous settings, and provide sampling algorithms.

</details>


### [132] [Ultrametric OGP - parametric RDT \emph{symmetric} binary perceptron connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19712)
*Mihailo Stojnic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文进一步连接参数化RDT与重叠间隙性质(OGPs)，针对对称二元感知机，通过分析联合边界程序获得ultrametric OGP的密度上界，发现与参数化RDT估计高度一致，并提出两者间存在同构关系的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究引入了fl-RDT框架来表征统计计算间隙(SCGs)，并在对称二元感知机中获得了算法阈值估计。本文旨在进一步连接参数化RDT与重叠间隙性质(OGPs)——解空间的另一关键几何特征，探索两者之间的深层联系。

Method: 考虑s级超度量OGPs(ult_s-OGPs)，开发包含组合和概率分量的分析联合边界程序：将组合部分转化为凸问题，概率部分转化为嵌套积分。通过数值评估获得ult_s-OGPs的约束密度上界，并与参数化RDT估计进行比较。

Result: 获得前两个级别的最紧边界：$\barα_{ult_1} \approx 1.6578$和$\barα_{ult_2} \approx 1.6219$，分别接近第3和第4提升级别的参数化RDT估计($α_c^{(3)} \approx 1.6576$和$α_c^{(4)} \approx 1.6218$)。在其他关键参数(包括重叠值和超度量簇的相对大小)上也观察到极好的一致性。

Conclusion: 基于观察结果，提出多个连接ult-OGP和参数化RDT的猜想：算法阈值$α_a$等于两个极限的极限值，且$α_{ult_s} \leq α_{c}^{(s+2)}$(可能对某些或所有s成立相等)。讨论了ult-OGP和参数化RDT所有关键参数间可能存在完全同构关系。

Abstract: In [97,99,100], an fl-RDT framework is introduced to characterize \emph{statistical computational gaps} (SCGs). Studying \emph{symmetric binary perceptrons} (SBPs), [100] obtained an \emph{algorithmic} threshold estimate $α_a\approx α_c^{(7)}\approx 1.6093$ at the 7th lifting level (for $κ=1$ margin), closely approaching $1.58$ local entropy (LE) prediction [18].
  In this paper, we further connect parametric RDT to overlap gap properties (OGPs), another key geometric feature of the solution space. Specifically, for any positive integer $s$, we consider $s$-level ultrametric OGPs ($ult_s$-OGPs) and rigorously upper-bound the associated constraint densities $α_{ult_s}$. To achieve this, we develop an analytical union-bounding program consisting of combinatorial and probabilistic components. By casting the combinatorial part as a convex problem and the probabilistic part as a nested integration, we conduct numerical evaluations and obtain that the tightest bounds at the first two levels, $\barα_{ult_1} \approx 1.6578$ and $\barα_{ult_2} \approx 1.6219$, closely approach the 3rd and 4th lifting level parametric RDT estimates, $α_c^{(3)} \approx 1.6576$ and $α_c^{(4)} \approx 1.6218$. We also observe excellent agreement across other key parameters, including overlap values and the relative sizes of ultrametric clusters.
  Based on these observations, we propose several conjectures linking $ult$-OGP and parametric RDT. Specifically, we conjecture that algorithmic threshold $α_a=\lim_{s\rightarrow\infty} α_{ult_s} = \lim_{s\rightarrow\infty} \barα{ult_s} = \lim_{r\rightarrow\infty} α_{c}^{(r)}$, and $α_{ult_s} \leq α_{c}^{(s+2)}$ (with possible equality for some (maybe even all) $s$). Finally, we discuss the potential existence of a full isomorphism connecting all key parameters of $ult$-OGP and parametric RDT.

</details>


### [133] [Adaptive MSD-Splitting: Enhancing C4.5 and Random Forests for Skewed Continuous Attributes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19722)
*Jake Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应MSD-Splitting（AMSD）方法，通过动态调整标准差乘数来改进MSD-Splitting，解决偏斜分布数据中的信息损失问题，并将其集成到随机森林中，在保持高效性的同时提升分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续数值属性的离散化是决策树归纳中的计算瓶颈。虽然MSD-Splitting技术通过均值和标准差分箱显著提高了C4.5算法的效率和精度，但其固定的一个标准差截断方法在处理高度偏斜分布数据（如生物医学和金融数据集）时会导致灾难性信息损失。

Method: 提出自适应MSD-Splitting（AMSD）方法：1）根据特征偏度动态调整标准差乘数；2）在密集区域缩小间隔以保持判别分辨率；3）将AMSD集成到集成方法中，特别是随机森林-AMSD（RF-AMSD）框架。

Result: 在Census Income、Heart Disease、Breast Cancer和Forest Covertype数据集上的实证评估表明：1）AMSD相比标准MSD-Splitting获得2-4%的精度提升；2）保持与O(N)时间复杂度的接近相同减少（相比O(N log N)的穷举搜索）；3）随机森林扩展以标准计算成本的一小部分实现了最先进的精度。

Conclusion: 自适应统计分箱在大规模集成学习架构中是可行的，AMSD方法在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了分类精度，特别是在处理偏斜分布数据时表现出优越性能。

Abstract: The discretization of continuous numerical attributes remains a persistent computational bottleneck in the induction of decision trees, particularly as dataset dimensions scale. Building upon the recently proposed MSD-Splitting technique -- which bins continuous data using the empirical mean and standard deviation to dramatically improve the efficiency and accuracy of the C4.5 algorithm -- we introduce Adaptive MSD-Splitting (AMSD). While standard MSD-Splitting is highly effective for approximately symmetric distributions, its rigid adherence to fixed one-standard-deviation cutoffs can lead to catastrophic information loss in highly skewed data, a common artifact in real-world biomedical and financial datasets. AMSD addresses this by dynamically adjusting the standard deviation multiplier based on feature skewness, narrowing intervals in dense regions to preserve discriminative resolution. Furthermore, we integrate AMSD into ensemble methods, specifically presenting the Random Forest-AMSD (RF-AMSD) framework. Empirical evaluations on the Census Income, Heart Disease, Breast Cancer, and Forest Covertype datasets demonstrate that AMSD yields a 2-4% accuracy improvement over standard MSD-Splitting, while maintaining near-identical O(N) time complexity reductions compared to the O(N log N) exhaustive search. Our Random Forest extension achieves state-of-the-art accuracy at a fraction of standard computational costs, confirming the viability of adaptive statistical binning in large-scale ensemble learning architectures.

</details>


### [134] [Benign Overfitting in Adversarial Training for Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19724)
*Jiaming Zhang,Meng Ding,Shaopeng Fu,Jingfeng Zhang,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次对简化ViT架构进行对抗训练的理论分析，证明在特定条件下ViT可实现近乎零的鲁棒训练损失和泛化误差，展现出良性过拟合现象


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管ViT在各种视觉任务中取得了显著成功，但研究表明它们与CNN一样容易受到对抗样本攻击。对抗训练是常用的防御策略，但其在ViT中的鲁棒性理论基础尚未得到充分探索

Method: 对简化ViT架构进行理论分析，研究在满足特定信噪比条件和适度扰动预算下，对抗训练对ViT鲁棒性的影响。通过理论推导证明鲁棒训练损失和泛化误差的收敛性

Result: 理论分析表明，在满足特定条件时，对抗训练可使ViT实现近乎零的鲁棒训练损失和泛化误差。即使在过拟合情况下也能保持强泛化能力，展现出良性过拟合现象。合成和真实数据集实验验证了理论发现

Conclusion: 首次为ViT对抗训练提供了理论分析框架，证明在适当条件下ViT可通过对抗训练实现鲁棒性，并展现出与CNN类似的良性过拟合现象，为理解ViT鲁棒性提供了理论基础

Abstract: Despite the remarkable success of Vision Transformers (ViTs) across a wide range of vision tasks, recent studies have revealed that they remain vulnerable to adversarial examples, much like Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs). A common empirical defense strategy is adversarial training, yet the theoretical underpinnings of its robustness in ViTs remain largely unexplored. In this work, we present the first theoretical analysis of adversarial training under simplified ViT architectures. We show that, when trained under a signal-to-noise ratio that satisfies a certain condition and within a moderate perturbation budget, adversarial training enables ViTs to achieve nearly zero robust training loss and robust generalization error under certain regimes. Remarkably, this leads to strong generalization even in the presence of overfitting, a phenomenon known as \emph{benign overfitting}, previously only observed in CNNs (with adversarial training). Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets further validate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [135] [FB-NLL: A Feature-Based Approach to Tackle Noisy Labels in Personalized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19729)
*Abdulmoneam Ali,Ahmed Arafa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FB-NLL：一种基于特征聚类的个性化联邦学习框架，通过谱结构分析实现一次性用户分组，并采用特征一致性策略处理噪声标签，显著降低通信开销并提升性能稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习动态的PFL方法容易受到低质量数据和噪声标签的影响，因为损坏的更新会扭曲聚类决策并降低个性化性能。需要一种能够解耦用户聚类与迭代训练动态、对噪声鲁棒的方法。

Method: FB-NLL采用特征中心框架：1）通过特征表示的协方差矩阵谱结构表征用户，利用子空间相似性一次性识别任务一致的用户分组；2）引入基于特征一致性的检测和校正策略，利用学习特征空间中的方向对齐和类特定特征子空间分配标签，无需估计随机噪声转移矩阵。

Result: 在多种数据集和噪声机制下的广泛实验表明，FB-NLL在平均准确率和性能稳定性方面持续优于现有最先进的基线方法，同时显著降低了通信开销和计算成本。

Conclusion: FB-NLL通过解耦用户聚类与训练动态、利用特征空间几何结构实现一次性聚类，并结合特征一致性处理噪声标签，为异构数据分布下的个性化联邦学习提供了一种高效、鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Personalized Federated Learning (PFL) aims to learn multiple task-specific models rather than a single global model across heterogeneous data distributions. Existing PFL approaches typically rely on iterative optimization-such as model update trajectories-to cluster users that need to accomplish the same tasks together. However, these learning-dynamics-based methods are inherently vulnerable to low-quality data and noisy labels, as corrupted updates distort clustering decisions and degrade personalization performance. To tackle this, we propose FB-NLL, a feature-centric framework that decouples user clustering from iterative training dynamics. By exploiting the intrinsic heterogeneity of local feature spaces, FB-NLL characterizes each user through the spectral structure of the covariances of their feature representations and leverages subspace similarity to identify task-consistent user groupings. This geometry-aware clustering is label-agnostic and is performed in a one-shot manner prior to training, significantly reducing communication overhead and computational costs compared to iterative baselines.
  Complementing this, we introduce a feature-consistency-based detection and correction strategy to address noisy labels within clusters. By leveraging directional alignment in the learned feature space and assigning labels based on class-specific feature subspaces, our method mitigates corrupted supervision without requiring estimation of stochastic noise transition matrices. In addition, FB-NLL is model-independent and integrates seamlessly with existing noise-robust training techniques. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets and noise regimes demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in terms of average accuracy and performance stability.

</details>


### [136] [FASTER: Value-Guided Sampling for Fast RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19730)
*Perry Dong,Alexander Swerdlow,Dorsa Sadigh,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FASTER是一种轻量级方法，通过将扩散策略的多候选动作采样建模为MDP，在去噪过程中早期预测并过滤候选动作，从而获得采样式测试时扩展的性能优势而无需计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前性能最好的强化学习算法使用测试时扩展方法（如采样多个动作候选并选择最佳）可能计算成本过高。需要一种方法既能获得扩散策略采样式测试时扩展的性能优势，又能避免其高昂的计算成本。

Method: 将多动作候选去噪和选择过程建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），目标是在去噪完成前逐步过滤动作候选。学习去噪空间中的策略和价值函数，预测去噪过程中动作候选的下游价值并进行过滤，同时最大化回报。该方法轻量级，可插入现有生成式RL算法。

Result: 在具有挑战性的长时程操作任务中，FASTER持续改进底层策略，在在线和批量在线RL中均取得最佳整体性能。应用于预训练的VLA时，FASTER在显著降低训练和推理计算需求的同时达到相同性能。

Conclusion: FASTER提供了一种高效的方法来获得扩散策略测试时扩展的性能优势，而无需承担传统采样方法的高计算成本，为生成式强化学习提供了实用的性能提升方案。

Abstract: Some of the most performant reinforcement learning algorithms today can be prohibitively expensive as they use test-time scaling methods such as sampling multiple action candidates and selecting the best one. In this work, we propose FASTER, a method for getting the benefits of sampling-based test-time scaling of diffusion-based policies without the computational cost by tracing the performance gain of action samples back to earlier in the denoising process. Our key insight is that we can model the denoising of multiple action candidates and selecting the best one as a Markov Decision Process (MDP) where the goal is to progressively filter action candidates before denoising is complete. With this MDP, we can learn a policy and value function in the denoising space that predicts the downstream value of action candidates in the denoising process and filters them while maximizing returns. The result is a method that is lightweight and can be plugged into existing generative RL algorithms. Across challenging long-horizon manipulation tasks in online and batch-online RL, FASTER consistently improves the underlying policies and achieves the best overall performance among the compared methods. Applied to a pretrained VLA, FASTER achieves the same performance while substantially reducing training and inference compute requirements. Code is available at https://github.com/alexanderswerdlow/faster .

</details>


### [137] [Safe Continual Reinforcement Learning in Non-stationary Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19737)
*Austin Coursey,Abel Diaz-Gonzalez,Marcos Quinones-Grueiro,Gautam Biswas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了安全持续强化学习，旨在解决在动态变化环境中同时保证安全约束和防止灾难性遗忘的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习控制器假设环境平稳，但在实际非平稳部署中，系统动态和操作条件会意外变化。同时，物理环境中的RL控制器必须在学习和执行阶段都满足安全约束，不允许在适应过程中出现瞬时违规。虽然持续RL和安全RL分别解决了非平稳性和安全性问题，但两者的交叉领域研究不足，因此需要研究能够在系统生命周期中适应变化同时保持安全的安全持续RL算法。

Method: 通过引入三个捕捉安全关键持续适应任务的基准环境，系统性地评估了来自安全RL、持续RL及其组合的代表性方法。研究了基于正则化的策略，这些策略部分缓解了安全约束与灾难性遗忘之间的权衡，并分析了它们的优势和局限性。

Result: 实证结果表明，在非平稳动态下，维持安全约束与防止灾难性遗忘之间存在根本性张力，现有方法通常无法同时实现这两个目标。基于正则化的策略部分缓解了这一权衡，但仍存在局限性。

Conclusion: 需要开发能够在变化环境中持续自主运行的安全、有弹性的学习型控制器。论文概述了关键开放挑战和研究方向，包括如何更好地平衡安全性与适应性，以及设计更有效的算法来解决安全持续RL中的基本张力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) offers a compelling data-driven paradigm for synthesizing controllers for complex systems when accurate physical models are unavailable; however, most existing control-oriented RL methods assume stationarity and, therefore, struggle in real-world non-stationary deployments where system dynamics and operating conditions can change unexpectedly. Moreover, RL controllers acting in physical environments must satisfy safety constraints throughout their learning and execution phases, rendering transient violations during adaptation unacceptable. Although continual RL and safe RL have each addressed non-stationarity and safety, respectively, their intersection remains comparatively unexplored, motivating the study of safe continual RL algorithms that can adapt over the system's lifetime while preserving safety. In this work, we systematically investigate safe continual reinforcement learning by introducing three benchmark environments that capture safety-critical continual adaptation and by evaluating representative approaches from safe RL, continual RL, and their combinations. Our empirical results reveal a fundamental tension between maintaining safety constraints and preventing catastrophic forgetting under non-stationary dynamics, with existing methods generally failing to achieve both objectives simultaneously. To address this shortcoming, we examine regularization-based strategies that partially mitigate this trade-off and characterize their benefits and limitations. Finally, we outline key open challenges and research directions toward developing safe, resilient learning-based controllers capable of sustained autonomous operation in changing environments.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [CCOpt: an Open-Source Solver for Large-Scale Mathematical Programs with Complementarity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18726)
*Anton Pozharskiy,François Pacaud,Moritz Diehl,Armin Nurkanović*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: CCOpt是一个基于MadNLP求解器的Julia软件包，专门用于求解带互补约束的数学规划问题，提供多种算法实现，并在多个领域展现出显著性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互补约束为工程优化中的非光滑函数和逻辑条件提供了强大的建模工具，但这类问题的非线性规划重构在所有可行点都违反经典正则性条件，使得理论分析和数值处理变得复杂，需要专门的算法来处理这种退化性。

Method: 基于非线性内点算法，实现松弛和惩罚方法工具箱，以及到最近提出的主动集方法的交叉。将松弛或惩罚参数与障碍参数耦合，提出单调和非单调的联合参数更新策略，并采用正则化技术改善KKT系统在小松弛参数下的条件数。

Result: 在经典MacMPEC基准测试、大规模安全约束最优潮流、非光滑系统最优控制以及模型预测控制中的二次规划互补约束问题上验证了实现。基准测试显示，相比其他方法（包括商业求解器），算法驱动的改进通常达到一个数量级。

Conclusion: CCOpt提供了一个高效的MPCC求解工具箱，通过创新的参数耦合策略和正则化技术，显著提升了求解性能，为工程优化中的互补约束问题提供了强大的计算工具。

Abstract: This paper presents the Julia package CCOpt, built on top of the interior-point solver MadNLP. CCOpt implements a suite of algorithms for Mathematical Programs with Complementarity Constraints (MPCCs). The solver additionally comes with interfaces for use in Matlab, Python, and C++. MPCCs have recently gained renewed attention in engineering optimization, as complementarity provides a powerful modeling tool for nonsmooth functions and logical conditions. These problems are inherently challenging since their nonlinear programming reformulations violate classical regularity conditions at all feasible points, complicating both theoretical analysis and numerical treatment. Consequently, specialized algorithms are required to handle this degeneracy, and several approaches have been proposed. We implement a toolbox of methods, including relaxation and penalty approaches, as well as a crossover to recently proposed active-set methods. Our solver is based on nonlinear interior-point algorithms that couple the relaxation or penalty parameter with the barrier parameter, yielding substantial speedups compared to standard implementations. Both monotone and nonmonotone strategies for updating this joint parameter update are proposed and investigated.In addition, we propose regularization techniques that improve the conditioning of the KKT system for small relaxation parameters, enhancing robustness and computational efficiency. The implementation is validated on the classical MacMPEC benchmark, large-scale problems in security-constrained optimal power flow, optimal control of nonsmooth systems, as well as on quadratic programs with complementarity constraints arising in model predictive control. This benchmarking reveals an algorithmically driven improvement of often an entire order of magnitude over other methods, including commercial solvers.

</details>


### [139] [A Dynamic Mode Decomposition Approach to Parameter Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18783)
*Moad Abudia,Opeyemi Owolabi,Joel A. Rosenfeld,Rushikesh Kamalapurkar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种数据驱动算法，用于控制仿射非线性系统的同时系统辨识和参数估计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决控制仿射非线性系统中未知参数的同时辨识问题，从状态-动作测量数据中恢复系统轨迹和参数值

Method: 使用状态-动作测量数据和已知参数值训练数据驱动预测模型，然后将该模型与未知参数对应的状态-动作数据结合来估计未知参数值

Result: 在具有未知阻尼、刚度和非线性系数的受控Duffing振子上进行数值实验，在开环激励下从数据中准确恢复了系统轨迹和未知参数值

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效实现控制仿射非线性系统的同时系统辨识和参数估计，为数据驱动的系统建模提供了可行方案

Abstract: This paper presents a data-driven algorithm for simultaneous system identification and parameter estimation in control-affine nonlinear systems. Parameter estimation is achieved by training a data-driven predictive model using state-action measurements and various known values at the parameters of interest. The predictive model is then used in conjunction with state-action data corresponding to unknown values of the parameters to estimate the said unknown value. Numerical experiments on the controlled Duffing oscillator with unknown damping, stiffness, and nonlinearity coefficients demonstrate accurate recovery of both the system trajectories and the unknown parameter values from data collected under open-loop excitation.

</details>


### [140] [Tropical low-rank approximation and application to optimal control of N-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18785)
*Marianne Akian,Stephane Gaubert,Shanqing Liu,Yang Qi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于轨迹的热带低秩张量方法，用于近似确定性最优控制问题的值函数，特别适用于N体系统，能在高维状态空间（达200维）中有效计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究确定性最优控制问题中值函数的近似方法，特别是针对具有固定初始状态的N体系统。这类问题的动作泛函包含局部动能项、势能项和相互作用势能，需要处理高维状态空间的计算挑战。

Method: 提出轨迹驱动的热带低秩张量近似方法：1）将值函数表示为热带张量（小秩上确界）；2）采用轨迹驱动而非全局传播基函数；3）沿相关轨迹序列逐步改进近似；4）保持基函数在子系统间的可加可分性；5）生成单调递增的可计算下界。

Result: 1）近似值函数形成对精确值函数的单调递增下界；2）热带张量秩随外层迭代次数至多线性增长；3）在初始状态和最优轨迹上，下界收敛到精确值；4）在N体系统中保持基函数的可加可分结构；5）数值实验显示方法在200维状态空间内半小时内有效。

Conclusion: 轨迹驱动的热带低秩张量近似方法为高维最优控制问题提供了有效的值函数近似方案，特别适用于N体系统，能在合理时间内处理高达200维的状态空间，同时保持理论收敛性保证。

Abstract: We study the approximation of the value function of deterministic optimal control problems with fixed initial state, motivated by \(N\)-body systems. In this setting, the action functional consists of local kinetic and potential terms, along with an interaction potential. We exploit this structure to approximate the value function using a tropical tensor of small rank, i.e.\ a supremum of a small number of additively separable functions. We propose a trajectory-based tropical low-rank approximation method. Rather than propagating basis functions globally, as in usual tropical numerical methods, the approximation of the value function is improved only along a sequence of relevant trajectories. The resulting approximations form a monotone family of computable lower bounds for the exact value function, with the tropical tensor rank increasing at most linearly with the number of outer iterations. Under suitable regularity assumptions, we show that at the initial state, and also at the optimal trajectory starting from this state, the lower bounds converge to the exact value. In the $N$-body setting, the generated basis functions remain additively separable across subsystems, thereby yielding a structured tropical low-rank approximation. Numerical experiments on $N$-body systems with Coulomb-type repulsion illustrate the effectiveness of the approach up to state dimension \(200\), within a half hour time budget.

</details>


### [141] [Target Mirror Descent: A Unifying Framework for Solving Monotone Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18813)
*Yu-Wen Chen,Can Kizilkale,Murat Arcak*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出目标镜像下降（TMD）框架，通过目标点校正机制稳定单调变分不等式中的镜像下降，统一多种经典算法并提供几何集成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 镜像下降在单调变分不等式问题上可能发散或循环，需要一种统一的框架来稳定单调流并统一多种经典算法。

Method: 提出目标镜像下降（TMD）框架，在双重更新中引入目标点校正机制，通过适当设计可恢复近端点算法、外梯度方法、分裂方法等多种经典算法。

Result: TMD框架统一了多种里程碑算法，修正了折扣镜像下降的均衡错位问题，并支持几何集成方法，使多个算法通过不同镜像映射并行求解同一问题。

Conclusion: TMD提供了一个统一的视角来理解多种经典算法的收敛性，其结构特征支持几何集成，为单调变分不等式问题的求解提供了灵活且理论保证的框架。

Abstract: It is well known that mirror descent may diverge or cycle on merely monotone variational inequalities. In this paper, we propose \emph{Target Mirror Descent} (TMD), a unified framework that stabilizes monotone flows via a target point correction mechanism in the dual update. By appropriate design choices, TMD recovers the proximal point algorithm, extragradient methods, splitting methods, Brown-von Neumann-Nash dynamics, forward-backward-forward dynamics, and discounted mirror descent as special cases. Thus, we establish a unified perspective on these landmark algorithms and their convergence. Beyond unification, we leverage the TMD framework to correct an equilibrium misalignment in discounted mirror descent and to generalize its higher-order extension beyond interior solutions. Moreover, a key structural feature of TMD is the explicit decoupling of the mirror map from the target determination, which enables \emph{geometric ensembles}: multiple algorithms solve the same problem in parallel using distinct mirror maps, while sharing a common dual update. We show that such an ensemble rigorously reduces to a single TMD with a synthesized mirror map, and thus inherits these convergence guarantees.

</details>


### [142] [A semi-smooth Newton method for the nonlinear conic problem with generalized simplicial cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18858)
*Nicolas F. Armijo,Yunier Bello Cruz,Gabriel Haeser*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种求解非线性锥规划问题的半光滑牛顿法，针对广义单纯锥（对称锥的线性映射像），建立锥投影方程并证明局部二次收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性锥规划问题在优化领域具有广泛应用，但现有方法在求解效率和理论分析方面存在局限。特别是对于广义单纯锥这类复杂锥结构，需要开发更有效的数值算法。

Method: 将Robinson法方程推广到锥规划设置，建立锥投影方程；提出半光滑牛顿迭代求解该方程；研究广义单纯锥的性质并证明其投影算子的强半光滑性。

Result: 建立了锥投影方程与KKT条件的等价性；证明了半光滑牛顿法的局部二次收敛性；在圆形锥规划和低秩矩阵补全问题上进行了数值实验验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的半光滑牛顿法为求解非线性锥规划问题提供了一种高效算法，特别适用于广义单纯锥结构，具有理论保证和实际应用价值。

Abstract: In this work we develop and analyze a semi-smooth Newton method for the general nonlinear conic programming problem. In particular, we study the problem with a generalized simplicial cone, i.e., the image of a symmetric cone under a linear mapping. We generalize Robinson's normal equations to a conic setting, yielding what we call the conic projection equations. The resulting system is equivalent to the KKT conditions associated with the nonlinear conic programming problem. A semi-smooth Newton iteration is proposed for solving it, and local quadratic convergence is established. We study properties of generalized simplicial cones and prove strong semi-smoothness of the projection operator onto them. Numerical experiments compare the method against a recent smoothing Newton approach on the circular cone programming problem, and we also apply it to the low-rank matrix completion problem.

</details>


### [143] [Capacity Expansion Planning for Puerto Rico's Electric Power System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18926)
*Elizabeth Glista,Tomas Valencia Zuluaga,Amelia Musselman,Minda Monteagudo,Juliette Franzman,Jean-Paul Watson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于波多黎各电力系统长期投资规划的数学优化框架，通过高分辨率容量扩展模型识别最低成本的发电和储能投资方案，以提升系统可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波多黎各电力系统面临可靠性挑战，需要制定长期投资规划来替代不可靠的旧有热力机组，满足未来负荷需求，并考虑可再生能源目标调整后的最优投资组合。

Method: 开发高分辨率容量扩展模型，采用节点传输建模（38kV及以上）、小时级时序运行、显式机组组合（考虑爬坡、最小启停时间、启动成本）、系统级燃料供应约束和随机运行场景。模型协同优化新投资和热力机组退役。

Result: 在波多黎各近期可再生能源目标放宽的背景下，最优投资组合需要在计划项目基础上新增至少1.5GW的H级联合循环机组。这些新增主要用于替代不可靠的旧有热力机组而非服务新增负荷，能够消除大系统负荷削减并恢复强健的备用裕度。

Conclusion: 研究为波多黎各电力系统长期规划提供了先进的建模框架，结果表明在考虑系统可靠性和经济性的前提下，新增联合循环容量是必要的投资选择，能够有效应对负荷变化、可再生能源波动和旧有机组高强迫停运率等挑战。

Abstract: This study presents a mathematical optimization framework and preliminary analysis for long-term investment planning in Puerto Rico's electric power system. We develop a high-resolution capacity expansion model to identify least-cost generation and storage investments that improve system reliability. The model co-optimizes new investments and thermal generator retirements while representing generator dispatch, unit commitment, fuel selection, and storage operations under constraints of equipment engineering limits, fuel supply limitations, and load satisfaction.
  Key methodological advances relative to prior long-term planning studies for Puerto Rico include: (i) nodal transmission modeling at 38 kV and above, (ii) hourly chronological operations for representative days, (iii) explicit unit commitment for existing and new thermal units with realistic ramping, minimum up and down times, and startup costs, (iv) system-wide fuel supply constraints, and (v) stochastic operating scenarios reflecting load variation, renewable availability, and the high forced outage rates of legacy units.
  Using data from LUMA, PREPA, DOE, and public sources, we build present-day (2024) and future (2030) test systems, with the latter including planned generation and storage projects. We evaluate planning scenarios that vary future load, fuel supply assumptions, realization of planned expansion, and allowable new technologies. Results show that, given the recent relaxation of interim renewable goals for the near future in Puerto Rico, an optimal portfolio includes at least 1.5 GW of new H-class combined cycle capacity beyond planned projects. These additions are needed mainly to replace unreliable legacy thermal units rather than to serve new load. The new combined cycle units eliminate modeled bulk-system load shedding and restore a strong reserve margin, even under stressed load and outage conditions.

</details>


### [144] [A Data-embedded Solution Paradigm for Nonconvex Probable Event Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18997)
*Qifeng Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出概率事件约束优化(PECO)新框架，不仅限制约束违反概率，还要求对超过阈值概率的所有事件都可行，保证解在所有高概率不确定性实现中有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机会约束仅限制约束违反概率，但无法保证解在所有高概率不确定性实现中都可行。需要新框架确保优化解在现实高概率场景中保持有效。

Method: 提出数据嵌入程序(DEP)，直接结合不确定参数的历史测量数据，为PECO提供确定性近似。利用机器学习在大数据时代估计解确定数据集族中的元素数量。

Result: PECO框架提供比传统机会约束更强的可行性保证。DEP方法为求解非线性和非凸PECO问题提供独特机会，不依赖凸性或线性假设。

Conclusion: PECO是优化不确定性的新框架，结合DEP和机器学习方法，能在大数据时代有效处理非线性非凸问题，提供更强的鲁棒性保证。

Abstract: This paper introduces a new modeling framework for optimization under uncertainty, called Probable Event Constrained Optimization (PECO). Unlike conventional chance-constrained formulations, which only limit the probability of constraint violation, PECO also explicitly requires feasibility for all events whose probability exceeds a prescribed threshold. This guarantees that solutions remain valid across all high-probability realizations of uncertainty. To solve PECO, we proposed a data-embedded program (DEP) which directly incorporates historical measurements of the uncertain parameters to obtain a deterministic approximation for PECO. While existing solution methods for optimization problems under uncertainty rely heavily on convexity or linearity assumptions, the proposed data-embedded solution paradigm provides a unique opportunity for solving nonlinear and nonconvex PECOs. The effectiveness of this approach depends on properly estimating the number of elements in the family of solution-determining data sets. As we enter the era of big data, this information can be properly estimated by leveraging the power of machine learning.

</details>


### [145] [Optimal Online and Offline Algorithms for Contextual MNL with Applications to Assortment and Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19008)
*Yunfan Zhang,Yuxuan Han,Hongyu Shan,Jose Blanchet,Zhengyuan Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了零售和电商中联合品类选择与定价的优化问题，在价格依赖的上下文多项logit模型下，提出了离线和在线算法，改进了现有理论边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售和电商运营中，在有限展示容量和不确定客户需求下，需要同时决定展示哪些产品及其价格。现有文献对品类优化和动态定价分别有成熟研究，但对两者联合优化的研究有限。

Method: 1. 构建价格依赖特征下的需求估计置信区域；2. 开发悲观的离线算法；3. 设计SupCB型在线算法；4. 提供计算更简单的Thompson采样替代方案。

Result: 1. 离线设置中建立了由最优品类-价格对周围局部信息控制的最优性保证；2. 在线设置中SupCB算法将最佳已知遗憾边界改进到$\widetilde{O}(W\sqrt{dT\log N}/L_0)$；3. 在仅品类或仅定价场景下，边界恢复了各自领域的近极小极大最优率。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了成熟品类优化/动态定价研究与有限联合优化文献之间的空白，为零售和电商中的联合决策问题提供了理论保证和实用算法。

Abstract: Selecting which products to display and at what prices is a central decision in retail and e-commerce operations. In many applications, these two choices must be made jointly under limited display capacity and uncertain customer demand. In this paper, we study the joint assortment and pricing problem under a price-based contextual multinomial logit model, where customer preferences depend on both product features and selling prices. Our analysis begins with the construction of a new confidence region for demand estimation under price-dependent features. Building on this result, we develop a pessimistic offline algorithm and SupCB-type online algorithms for joint assortment and pricing optimization. In the offline setting, we establish a suboptimality guarantee governed by local information around the optimal assortment-price pair, rather than by exact coverage of the optimal action. In the online setting, our SupCB-type algorithm improves the best previously known regret bound to $\widetilde{O}(W\sqrt{dT\log N}/L_0)$, and we also provide a computationally simpler Thompson-sampling alternative. When specialized to the assortment-only or pricing-only setting, our bounds recover the near-minimax-optimal rates established in those respective domains, thereby bridging the gap between the mature study on assortment optimization or dynamic pricing and the limited literature on their joint optimization.

</details>


### [146] [Exact Quadratic Penalty Function for Symplectic Eigenvalue Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19229)
*Jiaqi Wang,Nachuan Xiao,Xin Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于迹惩罚最小化的新方法求解对称正定矩阵的辛特征值问题，将约束优化转化为无约束最小化问题，并开发了结合BB步长和非单调线搜索的高效梯度算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辛特征值问题在量子力学和控制理论等多个科学领域至关重要，现有方法如黎曼梯度法在计算效率和收敛速度方面存在局限，特别是对于大规模问题。需要开发更高效、更通用的算法来解决这一重要数学问题。

Method: 1. 提出迹惩罚最小化方法，将辛特征值问题转化为无约束的迹惩罚函数最小化问题；2. 在温和条件下证明了惩罚问题与原约束优化问题的等价性；3. 开发了基于梯度法的算法，结合Barzilai-Borwein自适应步长规则和非单调线搜索技术。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提算法在计算效率和收敛速度上优于多种现有方法（包括黎曼梯度法），适用于稠密、稀疏和稀疏加低秩矩阵。特别在处理大规模辛特征值问题时显示出巨大潜力。

Conclusion: 迹惩罚最小化方法为求解辛特征值问题提供了一种高效、通用的解决方案。理论分析证明了方法的正确性，数值实验验证了算法的优越性能，特别是在大规模问题上的应用潜力。

Abstract: The symplectic eigenvalue problem for symmetric positive-definite (spd) matrices plays a crucial role in various scientific fields, including quantum mechanics and control theory. This paper introduces a trace-penalty minimization method, which transforms the symplectic eigenvalue problem into the unconstrained minimization of the trace-penalty function. We prove the equivalence between the penalty problem and the original constrained optimization problem under mild conditions, in the sense that the second-order stationary points of the trace-penalty function correspond to the solutions of the symplectic eigenvalue problem. Moreover, we develop an algorithm to minimize the trace-penalty function efficiently, which follows the scheme of gradient methods, together with the Barzilai-Borwein (BB) adaptive step-size rule and non-monotone line-search technique. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed algorithm outperforms a wide range of existing methods, such as Riemannian gradient-based methods, in terms of computational efficiency and convergence rate for dense, sparse, and sparse-add-low-rank matrices. These numerical results further demonstrate the great potential of our proposed algorithm, especially in solving large-scale symplectic eigenvalue problems.

</details>


### [147] [Sharp recovery and landscape guarantees for the nonconvex matrix LASSO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19381)
*Andrew D. McRae,Richard Y. Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文分析了在限制等距性(RIP)条件下，非凸分解矩阵LASSO问题的二阶临界点的统计最优性，特别关注了秩过参数化情况，揭示了核范数正则化的精确作用，并给出了秩过参数化并不总是改善优化景观的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管凸矩阵优化在RIP条件下能达到统计最优性，但对于大规模问题，通常采用非凸分解方法。然而，现有算法理论通常只能保证收敛到二阶临界点，缺乏对这些临界点统计性质的严格分析。特别是在秩过参数化情况下，需要理解核范数正则化的作用以及过参数化对优化景观的影响。

Method: 研究在RIP条件下，分解非凸矩阵LASSO（核范数正则化最小二乘估计）的二阶临界点。特别关注搜索秩r大于真实秩r*的过参数化情况。通过理论分析推导恢复误差界，揭示核范数正则化的精确作用。同时构造反例证明秩过参数化并不总是改善优化景观。所有结果推广到具有核范数正则化的任意凸函数在限制强凸性和光滑性条件下。

Result: 1. 获得了分解非凸矩阵LASSO二阶临界点的尖锐且统计最优的恢复误差界，这些界限精确刻画了核范数正则化的作用，在经典凸速率和无正则化非凸问题速率之间插值。2. 给出了反例证明秩过参数化并不总是改善优化景观，即使在RIP条件下。3. 建立了二阶临界点要么近似恢复凸问题的低秩近似最小值，要么精确恢复低秩全局最优解（如果存在）的充分条件。

Conclusion: 该研究为秩约束非凸方法的统计恢复能力提供了严格的理论基础，揭示了核范数正则化在过参数化情况下的重要作用，并挑战了秩过参数化总是有益的普遍观念。这些结果对理解非凸方法与凸方法在统计恢复能力方面的根本差异具有重要意义，特别是在计算扩展性较差的情况下。

Abstract: Low-rank matrix recovery can be solved to statistical optimality by convex matrix optimization under the classical assumption of restricted isometry property (RIP). However, for large problems, the convex formulation is commonly replaced by a smooth rank-constrained factored nonconvex problem for which algorithmic theory typically only guarantees convergence to second-order critical points. In this paper, we develop a sharp and statistically optimal theory for second-order critical points of the factored nonconvex matrix LASSO (nuclear-norm--regularized least-squares estimator) under RIP with particular emphasis on the overparametrized regime where the search rank $r$ exceeds the ground-truth rank $r_*$. Our recovery error bounds reveal the precise role of nuclear norm regularization, interpolating between the classical convex rate and known rates for the unregularized nonconvex problem. Complementing this positive result, we give examples showing that, contrary to popular belief, rank overparametrization does not always improve the optimization landscape even under RIP. This negative result raises questions about the fundamental statistical recovery capability of rank-constrained nonconvex approaches in comparison to convex approaches which have worse computational scaling. All of our results generalize to arbitrary convex functions with nuclear-norm regularization under restricted strong convexity and smoothness. In particular, we give sharp conditions under which second-order critical points of the nonconvex problem either (1) approximately recover low-rank approximate minima of the convex problem or (2) exactly recover a low-rank global optimum if one exists.

</details>


### [148] [Solving Convex-Concave Problems with $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4/(3p+1)})$ $p$th-Order Oracle Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19462)
*Lesi Chen,Xinliang Zhang,Chengchang Liu,Junru Li,Luo Luo,Jingzhao Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文改进了凸凹极小极大问题的高阶优化复杂度上界，并建立了新的下界，表明对于p≥2的情况仍存在理论间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论认为凸凹极小极大问题的最优复杂度为O(ε^{-2/(p+1)})，这是通过一阶最优方法的自然推广实现的。作者怀疑这个上界可能不是最优的，特别是对于高阶导数情况。

Method: 应用Monteiro-Svaiter加速技术来改进高阶优化算法的复杂度上界，同时建立了新的下界来评估最优性。

Result: 获得了改进的上界：Õ(ε^{-4/(3p+1)})，以及新的下界：Ω(ε^{-2/(3p-1)})。对于p≥2的情况，上下界之间存在间隙，表明仍有改进空间。

Conclusion: 该工作打破了之前被认为最优的复杂度上界，为高阶凸凹极小极大问题提供了更好的算法保证，同时揭示了理论上的未解问题。

Abstract: When the objective has Lipschitz continuous $p$th-order derivatives, it is known that convex-concave minimax problems can be solved with $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-2/(p+1)})$ $p$th-order oracle calls. This complexity upper bound was speculated to be optimal as it is achieved by a natural generalization of the optimal first-order method. In this work, we show an improved upper bound of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4/(3p+1)})$ by applying the Monteiro-Svaiter acceleration. We also establish a lower complexity bound of $Ω(ε^{-2/(3p-1)})$, suggesting a gap still exists for $p \ge 2$.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [On Solving the Multiple Variable Gapped Longest Common Subsequence Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18645)
*Marko Djukanović,Nikola Balaban,Christian Blum,Aleksandar Kartelj,Sašo Džeroski,Žiga Zebec*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于根节点状态图表示和迭代束搜索的框架，解决具有灵活间隙约束的VGLCS问题，在合成实例上验证了方法的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGLCS问题在分子序列比较和时间序列分析中有重要应用，需要处理连续字符间的灵活间隙约束，现有方法面临组合爆炸挑战。

Method: 基于根节点的状态图表示，将状态空间分解为多个根状态子图；采用迭代束搜索策略，动态维护有希望的候选根节点全局池；整合LCS文献中的启发式方法。

Result: 在320个合成实例（最多10个输入序列，最多500个字符）上进行了首次全面计算研究，实验结果显示该方法在可比运行时间内比基线束搜索更鲁棒。

Conclusion: 提出的框架有效解决了VGLCS问题，通过根节点状态图表示和迭代束搜索策略成功应对了组合爆炸挑战，为分子序列比较和时间序列分析提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses the Variable Gapped Longest Common Subsequence (VGLCS) problem, a generalization of the classical LCS problem involving flexible gap constraints between consecutive solutions' characters. The problem arises in molecular sequence comparison, where structural distance constraints between residues must be respected, and in time-series analysis where events are required to occur within specified temporal delays. We propose a search framework based on the root-based state graph representation, in which the state space comprises a generally large number of rooted state subgraphs. To cope with the resulting combinatorial explosion, an iterative beam search strategy is employed, dynamically maintaining a global pool of promising candidate root nodes, enabling effective control of diversification across iterations. To exploit the search for high-quality solutions, several known heuristics from the LCS literature are utilized into the standalone beam search procedure. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first comprehensive computational study on the VGLCS problem comprising 320 synthetic instances with up to 10 input sequences and up to 500 characters. Experimental results show robustness of the designed approach over the baseline beam search in comparable runtimes.

</details>


### [150] [Beyond One Output: Visualizing and Comparing Distributions of Language Model Generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18724)
*Emily Reif,Claire Yang,Jared Hwang,Deniz Nazar,Noah Smith,Jeff Heer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GROVE是一个交互式可视化工具，通过文本图展示语言模型的多个生成结果，帮助用户理解输出分布的结构特征，如共享模式、分支点和聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前用户通常通过单个输出来评估语言模型，但这隐藏了输出分布的结构特征（如模态、边缘案例、对微小提示变化的敏感性），导致用户从个别案例过度泛化。研究人员需要更好的工具来理解和分析语言模型的概率分布特性。

Method: 首先进行形成性研究（n=13）了解研究人员在实践中如何处理随机性、如何推理语言分布以及当前工作流程的痛点。然后开发GROVE系统，将多个语言模型生成结果表示为文本图中的重叠路径，揭示共享结构、分支点和聚类，同时保留对原始输出的访问。

Result: 通过三个众包用户研究（N=47、44和40）评估，结果显示混合工作流程最有效：图摘要改善了结构判断（如评估多样性），而直接输出检查在细节导向问题上表现更好。

Conclusion: GROVE通过可视化语言模型输出的分布结构，帮助用户更好地理解和分析模型的概率行为，支持混合工作流程：结合图摘要的结构洞察和直接输出的细节检查，为开放任务中的提示迭代提供更全面的视角。

Abstract: Users typically interact with and evaluate language models via single outputs, but each output is just one sample from a broad distribution of possible completions. This interaction hides distributional structure such as modes, uncommon edge cases, and sensitivity to small prompt changes, leading users to over-generalize from anecdotes when iterating on prompts for open-ended tasks. Informed by a formative study with researchers who use LMs (n=13) examining when stochasticity matters in practice, how they reason about distributions over language, and where current workflows break down, we introduce GROVE. GROVE is an interactive visualization that represents multiple LM generations as overlapping paths through a text graph, revealing shared structure, branching points, and clusters while preserving access to raw outputs. We evaluate across three crowdsourced user studies (N=47, 44, and 40 participants) targeting complementary distributional tasks. Our results support a hybrid workflow: graph summaries improve structural judgments such as assessing diversity, while direct output inspection remains stronger for detail-oriented questions.

</details>


### [151] [ARES: Adaptive Red-Teaming and End-to-End Repair of Policy-Reward System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18789)
*Jiacheng Liang,Yao Ma,Tharindu Kumarage,Satyapriya Krishna,Rahul Gupta,Kai-Wei Chang,Aram Galstyan,Charith Peris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARES框架通过"安全导师"系统发现并修复RLHF中的系统性漏洞，即核心LLM和奖励模型同时失效的问题，提升安全鲁棒性同时保持模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF对齐大语言模型时存在关键漏洞：不完美的奖励模型可能成为单点故障，当它无法惩罚不安全行为时。现有红队方法主要针对策略级弱点，忽略了核心LLM和奖励模型同时失效的系统性弱点。

Method: ARES采用"安全导师"动态组合语义连贯的对抗提示（结合主题、角色、策略、目标等结构化组件），生成对应的恶意和安全响应，同时暴露核心LLM和RM的弱点。利用发现的漏洞进行两阶段修复：先微调RM以更好检测有害内容，然后用改进的RM优化核心模型。

Result: 在多个对抗安全基准测试中，ARES显著增强了安全鲁棒性，同时保持了模型能力，为全面的RLHF安全对齐建立了新范式。

Conclusion: ARES框架通过系统性地发现和缓解核心LLM与奖励模型的双重漏洞，为RLHF安全对齐提供了更全面的解决方案，克服了传统红队方法只关注策略级弱点的局限性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) is central to aligning Large Language Models (LLMs), yet it introduces a critical vulnerability: an imperfect Reward Model (RM) can become a single point of failure when it fails to penalize unsafe behaviors. While existing red-teaming approaches primarily target policy-level weaknesses, they overlook what we term systemic weaknesses cases where both the core LLM and the RM fail in tandem.
  We present ARES, a framework that systematically discovers and mitigates such dual vulnerabilities. ARES employs a ``Safety Mentor'' that dynamically composes semantically coherent adversarial prompts by combining structured component types (topics, personas, tactics, goals) and generates corresponding malicious and safe responses. This dual-targeting approach exposes weaknesses in both the core LLM and the RM simultaneously. Using the vulnerabilities gained, ARES implements a two-stage repair process: first fine-tuning the RM to better detect harmful content, then leveraging the improved RM to optimize the core model. Experiments across multiple adversarial safety benchmarks demonstrate that ARES substantially enhances safety robustness while preserving model capabilities, establishing a new paradigm for comprehensive RLHF safety alignment.

</details>


### [152] [From Natural Language to Executable Narsese: A Neuro-Symbolic Benchmark and Pipeline for Reasoning with NARS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18873)
*Mina Gabriel,Pei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种神经符号推理框架，将自然语言推理问题转换为可执行的一阶逻辑和Narsese形式表示，并创建了NARS-Reasoning-v0.1基准数据集，通过语言结构化感知方法训练LLM生成推理相关的符号结构而非最终答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在语言生成方面表现出色，但在需要显式符号结构、多步推理和可解释不确定性的推理任务中仍不可靠。为了解决这一问题，需要将自然语言推理问题转化为可执行的符号表示形式，以构建更可靠的神经符号推理系统。

Method: 1) 开发神经符号框架，将自然语言推理问题翻译为一阶逻辑和Narsese可执行表示；2) 创建NARS-Reasoning-v0.1基准数据集，包含自然语言问题、一阶逻辑形式、可执行Narsese程序和三个黄金标签；3) 建立从一阶逻辑到可执行Narsese的确定性编译管道；4) 提出语言结构化感知方法，训练LLM生成推理相关的符号结构而非最终答案；5) 在Phi-2模型上训练LoRA适配器进行三标签推理分类。

Result: 1) 成功开发了从一阶逻辑到可执行Narsese的编译管道；2) 通过OpenNARS for Applications运行时执行验证了符号目标的行为对齐；3) 创建了包含可执行符号表示的基准数据集；4) 训练了Phi-2 LoRA适配器，展示了基准数据集支持监督适应的能力；5) 验证了基于执行的验证作为神经符号推理系统实用路径的可行性。

Conclusion: 可执行符号生成和基于执行的验证为构建更可靠的神经符号推理系统提供了实用路径。语言结构化感知方法使LLM能够生成推理相关的符号结构，而基准数据集支持监督适应和可执行评估，共同推动神经符号推理的发展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are highly capable at language generation, but they remain unreliable when reasoning requires explicit symbolic structure, multi-step inference, and interpretable uncertainty. This paper presents a neuro-symbolic framework for translating natural-language reasoning problems into executable formal representations using first-order logic (FOL) and Narsese, the language of the Non-Axiomatic Reasoning System (NARS).
